org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;\n\n/**\n * SaslServer implementation for SASL/SCRAM. This server is configured with a callback\n * handler for integration with a credential manager. Kafka brokers provide callbacks\n * based on a Zookeeper-based password store.\n *\n * @see <a href=\"https://tools.ietf.org/html/rfc5802\">RFC 5802</a>\n */\npublic class ScramSaslServer implements SaslServer {\n\n    private static final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(ScramSaslServer.class);\n    private static final Set<String> SUPPORTED_EXTENSIONS = Utils.mkSet(ScramLoginModule.TOKEN_AUTH_CONFIG);\n\n    enum State {\n        RECEIVE_CLIENT_FIRST_MESSAGE,\n        RECEIVE_CLIENT_FINAL_MESSAGE,\n        COMPLETE,\n        FAILED\n    }\n\n    private final ScramMechanism mechanism;\n    private final ScramFormatter formatter;\n    private final CallbackHandler callbackHandler;\n    private State state;\n    private String username;\n    private ClientFirstMessage clientFirstMessage;\n    private ServerFirstMessage serverFirstMessage;\n    private ScramExtensions scramExtensions;\n    private ScramCredential scramCredential;\n    private String authorizationId;\n    private Long tokenExpiryTimestamp;\n\n    public ScramSaslServer(ScramMechanism mechanism, Map<String, ?> props, CallbackHandler callbackHandler) throws NoSuchAlgorithmException {\n        this.mechanism = mechanism;\n        this.formatter = new ScramFormatter(mechanism);\n        this.callbackHandler = callbackHandler;\n        setState(State.RECEIVE_CLIENT_FIRST_MESSAGE);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @throws SaslAuthenticationException if the requested authorization id is not the same as username.\n     * <p>\n     * <b>Note:</b> This method may throw {@link SaslAuthenticationException} to provide custom error messages\n     * to clients. But care should be taken to avoid including any information in the exception message that\n     * should not be leaked to unauthenticated clients. It may be safer to throw {@link SaslException} in\n     * most cases so that a standard error message is returned to clients.\n     * </p>\n     */\n    @Override\n    public byte[] It may then flow to\n     * {@link #REAUTH_BAD_MECHANISM} before a transition to {@link #FAILED} if\n     * re-authentication is attempted with a mechanism different than the original\n     * one; otherwise it joins the authentication flow at the {@link #AUTHENTICATE}\n     * state and likewise ends at either {@link #COMPLETE} or {@link #FAILED}.\n     */\n    private enum SaslState {\n        INITIAL_REQUEST,               // May be GSSAPI token, SaslHandshake or ApiVersions for authentication\n        HANDSHAKE_OR_VERSIONS_REQUEST, // May be SaslHandshake or ApiVersions\n        HANDSHAKE_REQUEST,             // After an ApiVersions request, next request must be SaslHandshake\n        AUTHENTICATE,                  // Authentication tokens (SaslHandshake v1 and above indicate SaslAuthenticate headers)\n        COMPLETE,                      // Authentication completed successfully\n        FAILED,                        // Authentication failed\n        REAUTH_PROCESS_HANDSHAKE,      // Initial state for re-authentication, processes SASL handshake request\n        REAUTH_BAD_MECHANISM,          // When re-authentication requested with wrong mechanism, generate exception\n    }\n\n    private final SecurityProtocol securityProtocol;\n    private final ListenerName listenerName;\n    private final String connectionId;\n    private final Map<String, Subject> subjects;\n    private final TransportLayer transportLayer;\n    private final List<String> enabledMechanisms;\n    private final Map<String, ?> configs;\n    private final KafkaPrincipalBuilder principalBuilder;\n    private final Map<String, AuthenticateCallbackHandler> callbackHandlers;\n    private final Map<String, Long> connectionsMaxReauthMsByMechanism;\n    private final Time time;\n    private final ReauthInfo reauthInfo;\n    private final ChannelMetadataRegistry metadataRegistry;\n    private final Supplier<ApiVersionsResponse> apiVersionSupplier;\n\n    // Current SASL state\n    private SaslState saslState = SaslState.INITIAL_REQUEST;\n    // Next SASL state to be set when outgoing writes associated with the current SASL state complete\n    private SaslState pendingSaslState = null;\n    // Exception that will be thrown by `authenticate()` when SaslState is set to FAILED after outbound writes complete\n    private AuthenticationException pendingException = null;\n    private SaslServer saslServer;\n    private String saslMechanism;\n\n    // buffers used in `authenticate`\n    private Integer saslAuthRequestMaxReceiveSize;\n    private NetworkReceive netInBuffer;\n    private Send netOutBuffer;\n    private Send authenticationFailureSend = null;\n    // flag indicating if sasl tokens are sent as Kafka SaslAuthenticate request/responses\n    private boolean enableKafkaSaslAuthenticateHeaders;\n\n    public SaslServerAuthenticator(Map<String, ?> configs,\n                                   Map<String,               TransportLayer transportLayer,\n                                   Map<String, Long> connectionsMaxReauthMsByMechanism,\n                                   ChannelMetadataRegistry metadataRegistry,\n                                   Time time,\n                                   Supplier<ApiVersionsResponse> apiVersionSupplier) {\n        this.callbackHandlers = callbackHandlers;\n        this.connectionId = connectionId;\n        this.subjects = subjects;\n        this.listenerName = listenerName;\n        this.securityProtocol = securityProtocol;\n        this.enableKafkaSaslAuthenticateHeaders = false;\n        this.transportLayer = transportLayer;\n        this.connectionsMaxReauthMsByMechanism = connectionsMaxReauthMsByMechanism;\n        this.time = time;\n        this.reauthInfo = new ReauthInfo();\n        this.metadataRegistry = metadataRegistry;\n        this.apiVersionSupplier = apiVersionSupplier;\n\n        this.configs = configs;\n null || enabledMechanisms.isEmpty())\n            throw new IllegalArgumentException(\"No SASL mechanisms are enabled\");\n        this.enabledMechanisms = new ArrayList<>(new HashSet<>(enabledMechanisms));\n        for (String mechanism : this.enabledMechanisms) {\n            if (!callbackHandlers.containsKey(mechanism))\n                throw new IllegalArgumentException(\"Callback handler not specified for SASL mechanism \" + mechanism);\n            if (!subjects.containsKey(mechanism))\n                throw new IllegalArgumentException(\"Subject cannot be null for SASL mechanism \" + mechanism);\n            LOG.trace(\"{} for mechanism={}: {}\", BrokerSecurityConfigs.CONNECTIONS_MAX_REAUTH_MS_CONFIG, mechanism,\n                    connectionsMaxReauthMsByMechanism.get(mechanism));\n        }\n\n        // Note that the old principal builder does not support SASL, so we do not need to pass the\n        // authenticator or the transport layer\n        this.principalBuilder = ChannelBuilders.createPrincipalBuilder(configs, kerberosNameParser, null);\n\n        saslAuthRequestMaxReceiveSize = BrokerSecurityConfigs.DEFAULT_SASL_SERVER_MAX_RECEIVE_SIZE;\n    }\n\n    private void createSaslServer(String mechanism) throws IOException {\n        this.saslMechanism = mechanism;\n        Subject     saslServer = createSaslKerberosServer(callbackHandler, configs, subject);\n        } else {\n            try {\n                saslServer = Subject.doAs(subject, (saslServer == null) {\n                    throw new SaslException(\"Kafka Server failed to create a SaslServer to interact with a client during session authentication with server mechanism \" + saslMechanism);\n                }\n            } catch (PrivilegedActionException e) {\n                throw new SaslException(\"Kafka Server failed to create a SaslServer to interact with a client during session authentication with server mechanism \" + saslMechanism, e.getCause());\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    private SaslServer createSaslKerberosServer(final AuthenticateCallbackHandler saslServerCallbackHandler, final Map<String, ?> configs, Subject subject) throws IOException {\n        // server is using a JAAS-authenticated subject: determine service principal name and hostname from kafka server's subject.\n        final String servicePrincipal = SaslClientAuthenticator.firstPrincipal(subject);\n        KerberosName kerberosName;\n        try {\n            kerberosName = KerberosName.parse(servicePrincipal);\n        } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {\n            throw new KafkaException(\"Principal has name with unexpected format \" + servicePrincipal);\n        }\n        final String servicePrincipalName = kerberosName.serviceName();\n        final String serviceHostname = kerberosName.hostName();\n\n        LOG.debug(\"Creating SaslException(\"Kafka Server failed to create a SaslServer to interact with a client during session authentication\", e.getCause());\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Evaluates client responses via `SaslServer.evaluateResponse` and returns the issued challenge to the client until\n     * authentication succeeds or fails.\n     *\n     * The messages are sent and received as size delimited bytes that consists of a 4 byte network-ordered size N\n     * followed by N bytes representing the opaque payload.\n     */\n    @SuppressWarnings(\"fallthrough\")\n    @Override\n    public void authenticate() throws IOException {\n        if (saslState != SaslState.REAUTH_PROCESS_HANDSHAKE) {\n            if (netOutBuffer != null && !flushNetOutBufferAndUpdateInterestOps())\n                return;\n\n            if (saslServer != null && saslServer.isComplete()) {\n                setSaslState(SaslState.COMPLETE);\n                return;\n            }\n\n            // allocate on heap (as opposed to any socket server memory pool)\n            if (netInBuffer == null) netInBuffer = new NetworkReceive(saslAuthRequestMaxReceiveSize, connectionId);\n\n            try {\n                netInBuffer.readFrom(transportLayer);\n            } catch (InvalidReceiveException e) {\n                throw new SaslAuthenticationException(\"Failing SASL authentication due to invalid receive size\", e);\n            }\n            if (!netInBuffer.complete())\n                return;\n            netInBuffer.payload().rewind();\n        }\n        byte[] clientToken = new byte[netInBuffer.payload().remaining()];\n        netInBuffer.payload().get(clientToken, 0, clientToken.length);\n        netInBuffer = null; // reset the networkReceive as we read all the data.\n        try {\n            switch (saslState) {\n                case REAUTH_PROCESS_HANDSHAKE:\n                case HANDSHAKE_OR_VERSIONS_REQUEST:\n                case HANDSHAKE_REQUEST:\n                    handleKafkaRequest(clientToken);\n                    break;\n                case REAUTH_BAD_MECHANISM:\n                    throw new SaslAuthenticationException(reauthInfo.badMechanismErrorMessage);\n                case INITIAL_REQUEST:\n                    if (handleKafkaRequest(clientToken))\n                        break;\n                    // For default GSSAPI, fall through to authenticate using the client token as the first GSSAPI packet.\n                    // This is required for interoperability with 0.9.0.x clients which do not send handshake request\n                case AUTHENTICATE:\n                    handleSaslToken(clientToken);\n                    // When the authentication exchange is complete and no more tokens are expected from the client,\n                    // update SASL state. Current SASL state will be updated when outgoing writes to the client complete.\n                    if (saslServer.isComplete())\n                        setSaslState(SaslState.COMPLETE);\n                    break;\n                default:\n                    break;\n            }\n        } catch (AuthenticationException e) {\n            // Exception will be propagated after response is sent to client\n            setSaslState(SaslState.FAILED, e);\n        } catch (Exception e) {\n            // In the case of IOExceptions and other unexpected exceptions, fail immediately\n            saslState = SaslState.FAILED;\n            LOG.debug(\"Failed during {}: {}\", reauthInfo.authenticationOrReauthenticationText(), e.getMessage());\n            throw e;\n        }\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public KafkaPrincipal principal() {\n        Optional<SSLSession> sslSession = transportLayer instanceof SslTransportLayer ?\n                Optional.of(((SslTransportLayer) transportLayer).sslSession()) : Optional.empty();\n        SaslAuthenticationContext context = new SaslAuthenticationContext(saslServer, securityProtocol,\n                clientAddress(), listenerName.value(), sslSession);\n        KafkaPrincipal principal = principalBuilder.build(context);\n        if (ScramMechanism.isScram(saslMechanism) && Boolean.parseBoolean((String) saslServer.getNegotiatedProperty(ScramLoginModule.TOKEN_AUTH_CONFIG))) {\n            principal.tokenAuthenticated(true);\n        }\n        return principal;\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public Optional<KafkaPrincipalSerde> principalSerde() {\n        return principalBuilder instanceof KafkaPrincipalSerde ? @Override\n    public void handleAuthenticationFailure() throws IOException {\n        sendAuthenticationFailureResponse();\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public void close() throws IOException {\n        if (principalBuilder instanceof Closeable)\n            Utils.closeQuietly((Closeable) principalBuilder, \"principal builder\");\n        if (saslServer != null)\n            saslServer.dispose();\n        (a.name compare b.name) * -1\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  private[authorizer] def zkClientConfigFromKafkaConfigAndMap(kafkaConfig: KafkaConfig, configMap: mutable.Map[String, _<:Any]): prefixedValue.toString.trim)\n        }\n      }\n      zkClientConfig\n    }\n  }\n\n  private def validateAclBinding(aclBinding: AclBinding): Unit = {\n    if (aclBinding.isUnknown)\n      throw new /**\n     * The internal state transitions for initial authentication of a channel on the\n     * server side are declared in order, starting with {@link #INITIAL_REQUEST} and\n     * ending in either matchingAcls(resourceType: ResourceType, resourceName: String): AclSeqs = {\n    // this code is performance sensitive, make sure to run AclAuthorizerBenchmark after any changes\n\n    // save aclCache reference to a local val to get a consistent view of the cache during acl updates.\n    val aclCacheSnapshot = aclCache\n    val wildcard = aclCacheSnapshot.get(new ResourcePattern(resourceType, ResourcePattern.WILDCARD_RESOURCE, PatternType.LITERAL))\n      .map(_.acls.toBuffer)\n      .getOrElse(mutable.Buffer.empty)\n\n    val literal = aclCacheSnapshot.get(new ResourcePattern(resourceType, resourceName, PatternType.LITERAL))\n      .map(_.acls.toBuffer)\n      .getOrElse(mutable.Buffer.empty)\n\n    val prefixed = new ArrayBuffer[AclEntry]\n    aclCacheSnapshot\n      .from(new ResourcePattern(resourceType, resourceName, PatternType.PREFIXED))\n      .to(new ResourcePattern(resourceType, resourceName.take(1), PatternType.PREFIXED))\n      .forKeyValue { (resource, acls) =>\n        if (resourceName.startsWith(resource.name)) prefixed ++= acls.acls\n      }\n\n    new AclSeqs(prefixed, wildcard, literal)\n  }\n\n  private def matchingAclExists(operation: AclOperation,\n                                resource: ResourcePattern,\n                                principal: KafkaPrincipal,\n                                host: String,\n                                permissionType: AclPermissionType,\n                                acls: AclSeqs): Boolean = {\n    acls.find { acl =>\n      acl.permissionType == permissionType &&\n        (acl.kafkaPrincipal == principal || acl.kafkaPrincipal == AclEntry.WILDCARD_PRINCIPAL) &&\n        (operation == acl.operation || acl.operation == }\n\n  private[authorizer] def startZkChangeListeners(): Unit = {\n    aclChangeListeners = ZkAclChangeStore.stores\n      .map(store => store.createListener(AclChangedNotificationHandler, zkClient))\n  }\n\n  private def filterToResources(filter: ResourcePatternFilter): Set[ResourcePattern] = {\n    filter.patternType match {\n      case PatternType.LITERAL | PatternType.PREFIXED =>\n PatternType.LITERAL),\n          new ResourcePattern(filter.resourceType, filter.name, PatternType.PREFIXED))\n      case _ => throw new IllegalArgumentException(s\"Cannot determine matching resources for patternType $filter\")\n    }\n  }\n\n  private def logAuditMessage(requestContext: AuthorizableRequestContext, action: Action, authorized: Boolean): Unit = {\n    def logMessage: String = {\n SecurityUtils.resourceTypeName(action.resourcePattern.resourceType)\n      val resource = s\"$resourceType$RESOURCE_SEPARATOR${action.resourcePattern.patternType}$RESOURCE_SEPARATOR${action.resourcePattern.name}\"\n      val authResult = if (authorized) \"Allowed\" else \"Denied\"\n s\"Principal = $principal is $authResult Operation = $operation \" +\n        s\"from host = $host on resource = $resource for request = $apiKey with resourceRefCount = $refCount\"\n    }\n\n    if (authorized) {\n      // logIfAllowed is true if access is granted to the resource as a result of this authorization.\n      // In this case, log at debug level. If false, no access is actually granted, the result is used\n      // only to determine authorized operations. So log only at trace level.\n      if (action.logIfAllowed)\n        authorizerLogger.debug(logMessage)\n      else\n        authorizerLogger.trace(logMessage)\n    } else {\n      // logIfDenied is true if access to the resource was explicitly requested. Since this is an attempt\n      // to access unauthorized resources, log at info level. If false, this is either a request to determine\n      // authorized operations or a filter (e.g for regex subscriptions) to filter out authorized resources.\n      // In this case, log only at trace level.\n      if (action.logIfDenied)\n        authorizerLogger.info(logMessage)\n      else\n        authorizerLogger.trace(logMessage)\n    }\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Safely updates the resources ACLs by ensuring reads and writes respect the expected zookeeper version.\n    * Continues to retry until it successfully updates zookeeper.\n    *\n    * Returns a boolean indicating if the content of the ACLs was actually changed.\n    *\n    * @param resource the resource to change ACLs for\n    * @param getNewAcls function to transform existing acls to new ACLs\n    * @return boolean indicating if a change was made\n    */\n  private def updateResourceAcls(resource: ResourcePattern)(getNewAcls: Set[AclEntry] => Set[AclEntry]): Boolean = {\n    var currentVersionedAcls =\n      if (aclCache.contains(resource))\n        getAclsFromCache(resource)\n      else\n        getAclsFromZk(zkClient, resource)\n    var newVersionedAcls: VersionedAcls = null\n    var writeComplete = false\n    var retries = 0\n    while (!writeComplete && retries <= maxUpdateRetries) {\n      val newAcls = getNewAcls(currentVersionedAcls.acls)\n      val (updateSucceeded, updateVersion) =\n        if (newAcls.nonEmpty) {\n          if (currentVersionedAcls.exists)\n            zkClient.conditionalSetAclsForResource(resource, newAcls, currentVersionedAcls.zkVersion)\n          else\n            zkClient.createAclsForResourceIfNotExists(resource, newAcls)\n        } else {\n          trace(s\"Deleting path for $resource because it had no ACLs remaining\")\n          (zkClient.conditionalDelete(resource, currentVersionedAcls.zkVersion), 0)\n        }\n\n      if (!updateSucceeded) {\n        trace(s\"Failed to update ACLs for $resource. Used version ${currentVersionedAcls.zkVersion}. Reading data and retrying update.\")\n        Thread.sleep(backoffTime)\n        currentVersionedAcls = getAclsFromZk(zkClient, resource)\n        retries += 1\n      } else {\n        newVersionedAcls = VersionedAcls(newAcls, updateVersion)\n        writeComplete = updateSucceeded\n      }\n    }\n\n    if (!writeComplete)\n      throw new IllegalStateException(s\"Failed to update ACLs for $resource after trying a maximum of $maxUpdateRetries times\")\n\n    if (newVersionedAcls.acls != currentVersionedAcls.acls) {\n      info(s\"Updated ACLs for $resource with new version ${newVersionedAcls.zkVersion}\")\n      debug(s\"Updated ACLs for $resource to $newVersionedAcls\")\n      updateCache(resource, newVersionedAcls)\n      updateAclChangedFlag(resource)\n      true\n private def getAclsFromCache(resource: ResourcePattern): VersionedAcls = {\n    aclCache.getOrElse(resource, throw new IllegalArgumentException(s\"ACLs do not exist in the cache for resource $resource\"))\n  }\n\n\n  // Visible for benchmark\n  def updateCache(resource: ResourcePattern, versionedAcls: VersionedAcls): Unit = {\n    val currentAces: Set[AccessControlEntry] = aclCache.get(resource).map(_.acls.map(_.ace)).getOrElse(Set.empty)\n    val newAces: Set[AccessControlEntry] = versionedAcls.acls.map(aclEntry => aclEntry.ace)\n    val acesToAdd = newAces.diff(currentAces)\n    val acesToRemove = currentAces.diff(newAces)\n\n    acesToAdd.foreach { ace =>\n      val resourceTypeKey = ResourceTypeKey(ace, resource.resourceType(), resource.patternType())\n      resourceCache.get(resourceTypeKey) match {\n        case Some(resources) => resourceCache += (resourceTypeKey -> (resources + resource.name()))\n        case None => resourceCache += (resourceTypeKey -> immutable.HashSet(resource.name()))\n      }\n    }\n    acesToRemove.foreach { ace =>\n      val resourceTypeKey = ResourceTypeKey(ace, resource.resourceType(), resource.patternType())\n      resourceCache.get(resourceTypeKey) match {\n        case Some(resources) =>\n          val newResources = resources - resource.name()\n          if (newResources.isEmpty) {\n            resourceCache -= resourceTypeKey\n          } else {\n            resourceCache += (resourceTypeKey -> newResources)\n          }\n        case None =>\n      }\n    }\n\n    if (versionedAcls.acls.nonEmpty) {\n      aclCache = aclCache.updated(resource, versionedAcls)\n    } else {\n      aclCache -= resource\n    }\n  }\n\n  private def updateAclChangedFlag(resource: ResourcePattern): Unit = {\n    zkClient.createAclChangeNotification(resource)\n  }\n\n  private def backoffTime = {\n    retryBackoffMs + Random.nextInt(retryBackoffJitterMs)\n  }\n\n  private def apiException(e: Throwable): ApiException = {\n    e match {\n      case e1: ApiException => e1\n      case e1 => new ApiException(e1)\n    }\n  }\n\n  private[authorizer] def processAclChangeNotification(resource: ResourcePattern): Unit = {\n    lock synchronized {\n      val versionedAcls = getAclsFromZk(zkClient, resource)\n      info(s\"Processing Acl change notification for $resource, versionedAcls : ${versionedAcls.acls}, zkVersion : ${versionedAcls.zkVersion}\")\n      updateCache(resource, versionedAcls)\n    }\n  }\n\n processAclChangeNotification(resource)\n    }\n  }\n\n  private case class ResourceTypeKey(ace: AccessControlEntry,\n                                     resourceType: ResourceType,\n \\n95:     /**\\n96:      * The internal state transitions for initial authentication of a channel on the\\n97:      * server side are declared in order, starting with {@link #INITIAL_REQUEST} and\\n98:      * ending in either {@link #COMPLETE} or {@link #FAILED}.\\n99:      * <p>\\n100:      * Re-authentication of a channel on the server side starts with the state\\n101:      * {@link #REAUTH_PROCESS_HANDSHAKE}. It may then flow to\\n102:      * {@link #REAUTH_BAD_MECHANISM} before a transition to {@link #FAILED} if\\n103:      * re-authentication is attempted with a mechanism different than the original\\n104:      * one; otherwise it joins the authentication flow at the {@link #AUTHENTICATE}\\n105:      * state and likewise ends at either {@link #COMPLETE} or {@link #FAILED}.\\n106:      */\\n107:     private enum SaslState {\\n108:         INITIAL_REQUEST,               // May be GSSAPI token, SaslHandshake or ApiVersions for authentication\\n109:         HANDSHAKE_OR_VERSIONS_REQUEST, // May be SaslHandshake or ApiVersions\\n110:         HANDSHAKE_REQUEST,             // After an ApiVersions request, next request must be SaslHandshake\\n111:         AUTHENTICATE,                  // Authentication tokens (SaslHandshake v1 and above indicate SaslAuthenticate headers)\\n112:         COMPLETE,                      // Authentication completed successfully\\n113:         FAILED,                        // Authentication failed\\n114:         REAUTH_PROCESS_HANDSHAKE,      // Initial state for re-authentication, processes SASL handshake request\\n115:         REAUTH_BAD_MECHANISM,          // When re-authentication requested with wrong mechanism, generate exception\\n116:     }\\n117: \\n118:     private final SecurityProtocol securityProtocol;\\n119:     private final ListenerName listenerName;\\n120:     private final String connectionId;\\n121:     private final Map<String, Subject> subjects;\\n122:     private final TransportLayer transportLayer;\\n123:     private final List<String> enabledMechanisms;\\n124:     private final Map<String, ?> configs;\\n125:     private final KafkaPrincipalBuilder principalBuilder;\\n126:     private final Map<String, AuthenticateCallbackHandler> callbackHandlers;\\n127:     private final Map<String, Long> connectionsMaxReauthMsByMechanism;\\n128:     private final Time time;\\n129:     private final ReauthInfo reauthInfo;\\n130:     private final ChannelMetadataRegistry metadataRegistry;\\n131:     private final Supplier<ApiVersionsResponse> apiVersionSupplier;\\n132: \\n133:     // Current SASL state\\n134:     private SaslState saslState = SaslState.INITIAL_REQUEST;\\n135:     // Next SASL state to be set when outgoing writes associated with the current SASL state complete\\n136:     private SaslState pendingSaslState = null;\\n137:     // Exception that will be thrown by `authenticate()` when SaslState is set to FAILED after private SaslServer createSaslKerberosServer(final AuthenticateCallbackHandler saslServerCallbackHandler, final Map<String, ?> configs, Subject subject) throws IOException {\\n214:         // server is  * Evaluates client responses via `SaslServer.evaluateResponse` and returns the issued challenge to the client until\\n237:      * authentication succeeds or fails.\\n238:      *\\n239:      * The messages are sent and received as size delimited bytes that consists of a 4 byte network-ordered size N\\n240:      * followed by N bytes representing the opaque payload.\\n241:      */\\n242:     @SuppressWarnings(\\\"fallthrough\\\")\\n243:     @Override\\n244:     public void authenticate() throws IOException {\\n245:         if (saslState != SaslState.REAUTH_PROCESS_HANDSHAKE) {\\n246:             if (netOutBuffer != null && !flushNetOutBufferAndUpdateInterestOps())\\n247:                 return;\\n248: \\n249:             if (saslServer != null && saslServer.isComplete()) {\\n250:                 setSaslState(SaslState.COMPLETE);\\n251:                 return;\\n252:             }\\n253: \\n254:             // allocate on heap (as opposed to any socket server memory pool)\\n255:             if (netInBuffer == null) netInBuffer = new NetworkReceive(MAX_RECEIVE_SIZE, connectionId);\\n256: \\n257:             netInBuffer.readFrom(transportLayer);\\n258:             if (!netInBuffer.complete())\\n259:                 return;\\n260:             netInBuffer.payload().rewind();\\n261:         }\\n262:         byte[] clientToken = new byte[netInBuffer.payload().remaining()];\\n263:         netInBuffer.payload().get(clientToken, 0, clientToken.length);\\n264:         netInBuffer = null; // reset the networkReceive as we read all the data.\\n265:         try {\\n266:             switch (saslState) {\\n267:                 case REAUTH_PROCESS_HANDSHAKE:\\n268:                 case HANDSHAKE_OR_VERSIONS_REQUEST:\\n269:                 case HANDSHAKE_REQUEST:\\n270:                     handleKafkaRequest(clientToken);\\n271:                     break;\\n272:                 case REAUTH_BAD_MECHANISM:\\n273:                     throw new SaslAuthenticationException(reauthInfo.badMechanismErrorMessage);\\n274:                 case INITIAL_REQUEST:\\n275:                     if (handleKafkaRequest(clientToken))\\n276:                         break;\\n277:                     // For default GSSAPI, fall through to authenticate using the client token as the first GSSAPI packet.\\n278:                     // This is required for interoperability with 0.9.0.x clients which do not send handshake request\\n279:                 case AUTHENTICATE:\\n280:                     handleSaslToken(clientToken);\\n281: Current SASL state will be updated when outgoing writes to the client complete.\\n283:                     if (saslServer.isComplete())\\n284:                         setSaslState(SaslState.COMPLETE);\\n285:                     break;\\n286:                 default:\\n287:                     break;\\n288:             }\\n289:         } catch (AuthenticationException e) {\\n290:             // Exception will be propagated after response is sent to client\\n291:             setSaslState(SaslState.FAILED, e);\\n292:         } catch (Exception e) {\\n293:             // In the case of IOExceptions and other unexpected exceptions, fail immediately\\n294:             saslState = SaslState.FAILED;\\n295:             LOG.debug(\\\"Failed during {}: {}\\\", reauthInfo.authenticationOrReauthenticationText(), e.getMessage());\\n296:             throw e;\\n297:         }\\n298:     }\\n299: \\n300:     @Override\\n301:     public KafkaPrincipal principal() {\\n302:         Optional<SSLSession> sslSession = transportLayer instanceof SslTransportLayer ?\\n303:                 Optional.of(((SslTransportLayer) transportLayer).sslSession()) : Optional.empty();\\n304:         SaslAuthenticationContext context = new SaslAuthenticationContext(saslServer, securityProtocol,\\n305:                 clientAddress(), listenerName.value(), sslSession);\\n306:         KafkaPrincipal principal = principalBuilder.build(context);\\n307:         if (ScramMechanism.isScram(saslMechanism) && Boolean.parseBoolean((String) saslServer.getNegotiatedProperty(ScramLoginModule.TOKEN_AUTH_CONFIG))) {\\n308:             principal.tokenAuthenticated(true);\\n309:         }\\n310:         return principal;\\n311:     }\\n312: \\n313:     @Override\\n314:     public Optional<KafkaPrincipalSerde> principalSerde() {\\n315:         return principalBuilder instanceof KafkaPrincipalSerde ? }\\n327: \\n328:     @Override\\n329:     public void close() throws IOException {\\n330:         if (principalBuilder instanceof Closeable)\\n331:             Utils.closeQuietly((Closeable)    return transportLayer.socketChannel().socket().getInetAddress();\\n409:     }\\n410: \\n411:     private void handleSaslToken(byte[] clientToken) throws IOException {\\n412:         if authentication.\\\");\\n433:                 buildResponseOnAuthenticateFailure(requestContext, requestAndSize.request.getErrorResponse(e));\\n434:                 throw e;\\n435:             }\\n436:             if (!apiKey.isVersionSupported(version)) {\\n437:                 // We cannot create an error response if the request version of SaslAuthenticate is not supported\\n438:                 // This should not normally occur since clients typically check supported versions using ApiVersionsRequest\\n439:                 throw new UnsupportedVersionException(\\\"Version \\\" + version + \\\" is not supported for apiKey \\\" + apiKey);\\n440:             }\\n441:             /*\\n442:              * The client sends multiple SASL_AUTHENTICATE requests, and the client is known\\n443:              * to support the required version if any one of them indicates it supports that\\n444:              * version.\\n445:              */\\n446:             if               Utils.copyArray(saslAuthenticateRequest.data().authBytes()));\\n453:                 if (reauthInfo.reauthenticating() && saslServer.isComplete())\\n454: .setErrorCode(Errors.SASL_AUTHENTICATION_FAILED.code())\\n469:                                 .setErrorMessage(e.getMessage())));\\n470:                 throw e;\\n471:             } catch \\n492:     private boolean handleKafkaRequest(byte[] requestBytes) throws IOException, AuthenticationException {\\n493:         boolean isKafkaRequest = false;\\n494:         String clientMechanism =  * Evaluates client responses via `SaslServer.evaluateResponse` and returns the issued challenge to the client until\\n237:      * authentication succeeds or fails.\\n238:      *\\n239:      * The messages are sent and received as size delimited bytes that consists of a 4 byte network-ordered size N\\n240:      * followed by N bytes representing the opaque payload.\\n241:      */\\n242:     @SuppressWarnings(\\\"fallthrough\\\")\\n243:     @Override\\n244:     public void authenticate() throws IOException {\\n245:         if (saslState != SaslState.REAUTH_PROCESS_HANDSHAKE) {\\n246:             if (netOutBuffer != null && !flushNetOutBufferAndUpdateInterestOps())\\n247:                 return;\\n248: \\n249:             if (saslServer != null && saslServer.isComplete()) {\\n250:                 setSaslState(SaslState.COMPLETE);\\n251:                 return;\\n252:             }\\n253: \\n254:             // allocate on heap (as opposed to any socket server memory pool)\\n255: data.\\n265:         try {\\n266:             switch (saslState) {\\n267:                 case REAUTH_PROCESS_HANDSHAKE:\\n268:                 case HANDSHAKE_OR_VERSIONS_REQUEST:\\n269: }\\n327: \\n328:     @Override\\n329:     public void close() throws IOException {\\n330:         if (principalBuilder instanceof Closeable)\\n331:             Utils.closeQuietly((Closeable) \\n492:     private boolean handleKafkaRequest(byte[] requestBytes) throws IOException, AuthenticationException {\\n493:         boolean isKafkaRequest = false;\\n494:         String clientMechanism = null;\\n495:         try {\\n496:             ByteBuffer requestBuffer = ByteBuffer.wrap(requestBytes);\\n497:             RequestHeader header = RequestHeader.parse(requestBuffer);\\n498: (LOG.isDebugEnabled()) {\\n527:                     StringBuilder tokenBuilder = new StringBuilder();\\n528:                     for (byte b : requestBytes) {\\n529:                LOG.debug(\\\"Received client packet of length {} starting with bytes 0x{}, process as GSSAPI packet\\\", requestBytes.length, tokenBuilder);\\n534:                 }\\n535: .getNegotiatedProperty(SaslInternalConfigs.CREDENTIAL_LIFETIME_MS_SASL_NEGOTIATED_PROPERTY_KEY);\\n675:             Long connectionsMaxReauthMs =          retvalSessionLifetimeMs);\\n695:                 else\\n696:                     LOG.debug(\\n697:                             \\\"Authentication complete; session max lifetime from broker /**\n96:      * The internal state transitions for initial authentication of a channel on the\n97:      * server side are declared in order, starting with {@link #INITIAL_REQUEST} and\n98:      * ending in either {@link #COMPLETE} or {@link #FAILED}.\n99:      * <p>\n100:      * Re-authentication of a channel on the server side starts with the state\n101:      * {@link It may then flow to\n102:      * {@link #REAUTH_BAD_MECHANISM} before a transition to {@link #FAILED} if\n103:      * re-authentication is attempted with a mechanism different than the original\n104:      * one; otherwise it joins the authentication flow at the {@link #AUTHENTICATE}\n105:      * state and likewise ends at either {@link #COMPLETE} or {@link #FAILED}.\n106:      */\n107:     private Authentication tokens (SaslHandshake v1 and above indicate SaslAuthenticate headers)\n112:         COMPLETE,                      // Authentication completed successfully\n113:         FAILED,                        // Authentication failed\n114:         REAUTH_PROCESS_HANDSHAKE,      // Initial state for re-authentication, processes SASL handshake request\n115:         REAUTH_BAD_MECHANISM,          // When re-authentication requested with wrong mechanism, generate exception\n116:     }\n117: \n118:     private final SecurityProtocol securityProtocol;\n119:     private final ListenerName } catch (PrivilegedActionException e) {\n231:             throw new SaslException(\"Kafka Server failed to create a SaslServer to interact with a client during session authentication\",  * authentication succeeds or fails.\n238:      *\n239:      * The messages are sent and received as size delimited bytes that consists of a 4 byte network-ordered size N\n240:      * followed by N bytes representing the opaque payload.\n241:      */\n242:     @SuppressWarnings(\"fallthrough\")\n243:     @Override\n244:     public void authenticate() throws IOException {\n245:         if (saslState != SaslState.REAUTH_PROCESS_HANDSHAKE) {\n246:             if (netOutBuffer != null && !flushNetOutBufferAndUpdateInterestOps())\n247:                 return;\n248: \n249:             if (saslServer != null && saslServer.isComplete()) {\n250:                 setSaslState(SaslState.COMPLETE);\n251:                 return;\n252:             }\n253: \n254:             // allocate on heap (as opposed to any socket server memory pool)\n255:             if (netInBuffer == null) netInBuffer = new NetworkReceive(MAX_RECEIVE_SIZE, connectionId);\n256: \n257:             netInBuffer.readFrom(transportLayer);\n258:             if (!netInBuffer.complete())\n259:                 return;\n260:             netInBuffer.payload().rewind();\n261:         }\n262:         byte[] clientToken = new byte[netInBuffer.payload().remaining()];\n263:         netInBuffer.payload().get(clientToken, 0, clientToken.length);\n264:         netInBuffer = null; // reset the networkReceive as we read all the data.\n265:         try {\n266:             switch (saslState) {\n267:                 case REAUTH_PROCESS_HANDSHAKE:\n268:                 case packet.\n278:                     // This is required for interoperability with 0.9.0.x clients which do not send handshake request\n279:                 case AUTHENTICATE:\n280: }\\n619: \\n620:   /**\\n621:     * Safely updates the resources ACLs by ensuring reads and writes respect the expected zookeeper version.\\n622:     * Continues to retry until it successfully updates * @param getNewAcls function to transform existing acls to new ACLs\\n628:     * @return boolean indicating if a change was made\\n629:     */\\n630:   private def updateResourceAcls(resource: ResourcePattern)(getNewAcls: Set[AclEntry] => Set[AclEntry]): Boolean = {\\n631:     var currentVersionedAcls =\\n632:       if (aclCache.contains(resource))\\n633:         getAclsFromCache(resource)\\n634:       else\\n635:         getAclsFromZk(resource)\\n636:     var newVersionedAcls: VersionedAcls = null\\n637:     var writeComplete = false\\n638:     var retries = 0\\n639:     while (!writeComplete && retries <= maxUpdateRetries) {\\n640:       val newAcls = getNewAcls(currentVersionedAcls.acls)\\n641:       val (updateSucceeded, updateVersion) =\\n642:         if (newAcls.nonEmpty) {\\n643:           if (currentVersionedAcls.exists)\\n644:             zkClient.conditionalSetAclsForResource(resource, newAcls, currentVersionedAcls.zkVersion)\\n645:           else\\n646:             zkClient.createAclsForResourceIfNotExists(resource, newAcls)\\n647:         } else {\\n648:           trace(s\\\"Deleting path for $resource because it had no ACLs remaining\\\")\\n649:           (zkClient.conditionalDelete(resource, currentVersionedAcls.zkVersion), 0)\\n650:         }\\n651: \\n652:       if (!updateSucceeded) {\\n653:         trace(s\\\"Failed to update ACLs for $resource. Used version ${currentVersionedAcls.zkVersion}.       newVersionedAcls = VersionedAcls(newAcls, updateVersion)\\n659:         writeComplete = updateSucceeded\\n660:       }\\n661:     }\\n662: \\n663:     if (!writeComplete)\\n664:       throw new IllegalStateException(s\\\"Failed to update ACLs for $resource after trying a maximum of $maxUpdateRetries times\\\")\\n665: \\n666:     if (newVersionedAcls.acls != currentVersionedAcls.acls) {\\n667:       info(s\\\"Updated ACLs for $resource with new version ${newVersionedAcls.zkVersion}\\\")\\n668:       debug(s\\\"Updated ACLs for $resource to $newVersionedAcls\\\")\\n669: aclCache.get(resource).map(_.acls.map(_.ace)).getOrElse(Set.empty)\\n690:     val newAces: Set[AccessControlEntry] = versionedAcls.acls.map(aclEntry => aclEntry.ace)\\n691:     val acesToAdd = \n202:   override def createAcls(requestContext: AuthorizableRequestContext,\n203:                           aclBindings: util.List[AclBinding]): util.List[_ <: CompletionStage[AclCreateResult]] = updates.\n245:    *   The returned [[org.apache.kafka.server.authorizer.AclDeleteResult]] indicates which ACLs were deleted.</li>\n246:    *   <li>If the provided filters use resource pattern \n320:   override def authorizeByResourceType(requestContext: AuthorizableRequestContext,\n321:                                        op: AclOperation,\n322: \n378:   private def matchingResources(resourceSnapshot: immutable.Map[ResourceTypeKey, immutable.Set[String]],\n379:                                 principal: String, host: String, op: AclOperation, private def hasMatchingResources(resourceSnapshot: immutable.Map[ResourceTypeKey, immutable.Set[String]],\n396:                                    principal: String, host: String, op: AclOperation, \n412: \n413:   private def allowAny(allowLiterals: ArrayBuffer[immutable.Set[String]], allowPrefixes: ArrayBuffer[immutable.Set[String]],\n414:                        denyLiterals: }\n472: \n473:     def allowAclExists(acls: AclSeqs): Boolean = {\n474:       // Check if there are any Allow ACLs which would allow this operation.\n475:       // Allowing read, write, delete, or }\n491: \n492:     // Evaluate if operation is allowed\n493:     val authorized = isSuperUser(principal) || aclsAllowAccess\n494: \n495:     logAuditMessage(requestContext, action, authorized)\n496:     if (authorized) AuthorizationResult.ALLOWED else AuthorizationResult.DENIED\n497:   }\n498: \n499:   def isSuperUser(principal: KafkaPrincipal): Boolean = {\n500:     if false\n504:   }\n505: \n506:   @nowarn(\"cat=deprecation\")\n507:   private def matchingAcls(resourceType: ResourceType, resourceName: String): AclSeqs = {\n508:     // this code is performance \n574:   private def filterToResources(filter: ResourcePatternFilter): Set[ResourcePattern] = {\n575:     filter.patternType match {\n576:       case PatternType.LITERAL | PatternType.PREFIXED = {\n586:     def logMessage: String = {\n587:       val principal = requestContext.principal\n588:       val operation = SecurityUtils.operationName(action.operation)\n589:       val host = requestContext.clientAddress.getHostAddress\n590:       val resourceType = SecurityUtils.resourceTypeName(action.resourcePattern.resourceType)\n591:       val resource = s\"$resourceType$ResourceSeparator${action.resourcePattern.patternType}$ResourceSeparator${action.resourcePattern.name}\"\n592:       val authResult = if (authorized) \"Allowed\" else \"Denied\"\n593:       val apiKey = if (ApiKeys.hasId(requestContext.requestType)) ApiKeys.forId(requestContext.requestType).name else requestContext.requestType\n594:       val refCount = action.resourceReferenceCount\n595: \n596:       s\"Principal = $principal is $authResult Operation = $operation \" +\n597:         s\"from host = $host on resource = $resource for request = $apiKey   // In this case, log at debug level. If false, no access is actually granted, the result is used\n603:       // only to determine authorized operations. So log only at trace level.\n604:       if (action.logIfAllowed)\n605:         authorizerLogger.debug(logMessage)\n606:       else\n607:         authorizerLogger.trace(logMessage)\n608:     } else {\n609:       // logIfDenied is true if access to the resource was explicitly requested. Since this is an attempt\n610:       // to access unauthorized resources, log at info level. If false, this is either a request to determine\n611:       // authorized operations or a filter (e.g for regex subscriptions) to filter out authorized resources.\n612:       // In this case, log only at trace level.\n613:       if (action.logIfDenied)\n614:         authorizerLogger.info(logMessage)\n615:       else\n616:         authorizerLogger.trace(logMessage)\n617:     }\n618:   }\n619: \n620:   /**\n621:     * Safely updates the resources ACLs by ensuring reads and writes respect the expected zookeeper version.\n622:     * Continues to retry until it successfully updates zookeeper.\n623:     *\n624:     * Returns a boolean indicating if the content of the ACLs was actually changed.\n625:     *\n626:     * @param resource the resource to change ACLs for\n627:     * @param getNewAcls function to transform existing acls to new ACLs\n628:     * @return boolean indicating if a change was made\n629:     */\n630:   private def updateResourceAcls(resource: ResourcePattern)(getNewAcls: Set[AclEntry] => Set[AclEntry]): Boolean = {\n631:     var currentVersionedAcls =\n632:       if (aclCache.contains(resource))\n633:         getAclsFromCache(resource)\n634:       else\n635:         getAclsFromZk(resource)\n636:     var newVersionedAcls: VersionedAcls = null\n637:     var writeComplete = false\n638:     var retries = 0\n639:     while (!writeComplete && retries <= maxUpdateRetries) {\n640:       val newAcls = getNewAcls(currentVersionedAcls.acls)\n641:       val (updateSucceeded, updateVersion) =\n642:         if (newAcls.nonEmpty) {\n643:           if (currentVersionedAcls.exists)\n644:             zkClient.conditionalSetAclsForResource(resource, newAcls, currentVersionedAcls.zkVersion)\n645:           else\n646:             zkClient.createAclsForResourceIfNotExists(resource, newAcls)\n647:         } else {\n648:           trace(s\"Deleting path for $resource because it had no ACLs remaining\")\n649:           (zkClient.conditionalDelete(resource, currentVersionedAcls.zkVersion), 0)\n650:         }\n651: \n652:       if (!updateSucceeded) {\n653:         trace(s\"Failed to update ACLs for $resource. Used version ${currentVersionedAcls.zkVersion}. Reading data and retrying update.\")\n654:         Thread.sleep(backoffTime)\n655:         currentVersionedAcls = getAclsFromZk(resource)\n656:         retries += 1\n657:       } else {\n658:         newVersionedAcls = VersionedAcls(newAcls, updateVersion)\n659:         writeComplete = updateSucceeded\n660:       }\n661:     }\n662: \n663:     if (!writeComplete)\n664:       throw new IllegalStateException(s\"Failed to update ACLs for $resource after trying a maximum of $maxUpdateRetries times\")\n665: \n666:     if (newVersionedAcls.acls != currentVersionedAcls.acls) {\n667:       info(s\"Updated ACLs for $resource with new version ${newVersionedAcls.zkVersion}\")\n668:       debug(s\"Updated ACLs for $resource to $newVersionedAcls\")\n669:       updateCache(resource, newVersionedAcls)\n670:       updateAclChangedFlag(resource)\n671:       true\n672:     } else {\n673:       debug(s\"Updated ACLs for $resource, no change was made\")\n674:       updateCache(resource, newVersionedAcls) // Even if no change, update the version\n675:       false\n676:     }\n677:   }\n678: \n679:   private def getAclsFromCache(resource: ResourcePattern):
